\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510        \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510        \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n   \u2502 user_api_keys (+5 BYOK    \u2502        \u2502  avatar_reply_log (X engine, \u2502        \u2502   mcorch_nodes (mesh)   \u2502\n   \u2502 cols, Vault-encrypted)    \u2502        \u2502  pay-per-use ledger + dedup) \u2502        \u2502  observation node no    \u2502\n   \u2502 hedra/omnihuman/eleven/   \u2502        \u2502  per-user, RLS own           \u2502        \u2502  1\u00ba render bem-sucedido \u2502\n   \u2502 cartesia (+higgsfield\u2713)   \u2502        \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518        \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n   \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n```\n\n**Entities (cardinalidade-chave):**\n\n- **`avatar_identities` (NET-NEW, biometric PII):** rosto-lock per-user \u2014 ref de LoRA weights/Soul ID em storage isolado + embedding can\u00f4nico ArcFace + `persona_type` (self_clone/fictional) + `verification_passed` + `arcface_threshold` calibrado. RLS own. 1 user \u2192 N identidades. Origem do `input_face_identity` do pipeline (SDD \u00a71.2).\n- **`voice_profiles` (NET-NEW, biometric PII):** voz clonada per-user \u2014 ref de voiceprint cifrado no Vault + provider (elevenlabs/cartesia) + language (en/pt-BR, sem code-switch \u2014 FM-AC-013). RLS own. 1 user \u2192 N perfis. Origem do `input_voice_profile`.\n- **`avatar_consents` (NET-NEW, imut\u00e1vel):** artefato de consentimento por finalidade biom\u00e9trica (train_lora / face_embedding / voice_clone) \u2014 vers\u00e3o do termo + timestamp + IP + `consent_granted` + `revoked_at` (LGPD Art. 11). Imut\u00e1vel exceto `revoked_at`. Gate fail-closed de todo render.\n- **`video_renders` (REUSO + ALTER):** ledger de render \u2014 ganha `composition.avatar` jsonb e relaxa o CHECK `engine` para `+hedra/omnihuman/kling`. \u00c9 o ledger biom\u00e9trico-de-render, N\u00c3O uma tabela nova (OTD-AC-005).\n- **`creative_assets` (REUSO):** spine de assets \u2014 `source_module='faceless'`, `kind \u2208 {video,audio}`, `parent_asset_id` para lineage (voz \u2192 talking-head \u2192 enhance).\n- **`channel_variants` / `autopilot_plans` / `autopilot_cycles` (REUSO):** repurpose nativo 1\u2192N + jun\u00e7\u00e3o de cad\u00eancia 30d (Fatia 8). `autopilot_plans.platforms` CHECK relaxado para `+youtube/tiktok/instagram/pinterest`.\n- **`user_api_keys` (REUSO + ALTER):** view Vault-mascarada \u2014 ganha 4 colunas BYOK cifradas (`hedra_api_key`, `omnihuman_api_key`, `elevenlabs_api_key`\u2713j\u00e1-existe, `cartesia_api_key`); `higgsfield_api_key` J\u00c1 EXISTE (migration `20260527005356`) \u2192 reusar.\n- **`avatar_reply_log` (NET-NEW):** ledger pay-per-use do reply engine do X (FR-AC-042..045) \u2014 dedup 24h UTC + custo por reply (read/write/com-link) + cap per-user. RLS own.\n- **`mcorch_nodes` (REUSO):** Knowledge Mesh \u2014 n\u00f3 de observa\u00e7\u00e3o `node_type='avatar_render_observation'` no 1\u00ba render bem-sucedido (Mesh Connection Mandate).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Logical Data Model\n\n### Table: `avatar_identities` (NET-NEW \u2014 biometric PII, RLS own)\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | FK | Description |\n|--------|------|----------|---------|-----|------------|\n| `id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | \u2014 | Primary key |\n| `user_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | `auth.users.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | Tenant isolation (RLS own) |\n| `project_id` | `uuid` | NULL | \u2014 | `projects.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | Project scope (opcional) |\n| `persona_type` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('self_clone','fictional')` \u2014 dirige templates de compliance (FR-AC-030/032) |\n| `tier` | `smallint` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `CHECK IN (1,2,3)` \u2014 ladder de identidade (Tier3 = LoRA can\u00f4nico) |\n| `identity_storage_key` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | LoRA/Soul-ID weights ref no bucket privado, prefixo `user_id/` |\n| `provider` | `text` | NOT NULL | `'higgsfield'` | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('higgsfield','lora_flux')` \u2014 motor de identity-lock |\n| `arcface_threshold` | `numeric` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Limiar do Identity Gate **calibrado FAR/FRR/EER** \u2014 config, NUNCA hardcode 0.95/0.7-0.8 (OTD-AC-004) |\n| `verification_passed` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | `false` | \u2014 | `true` s\u00f3 ap\u00f3s selfie-liveness bater (self-clone \u2014 FR-AC-032); artefato n\u00e3o persiste em falha |\n| `commercial_license_ok` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | `false` | \u2014 | Gate FM-AC-001 \u2014 bloqueia pesos non-commercial (FLUX.1-dev) |\n| `embedding` | `vector(768)` | NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Mesh embedding can\u00f4nico (trigger `embed-mcorch-node`) |\n| `status` | `text` | NOT NULL | `'training'` | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('training','active','revoked')` |\n| `last_used_at` | `timestamptz` | NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Drive do retention-sweep por inatividade (FR-AC-028) |\n| `created_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | \u2014 | Audit |\n| `updated_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | \u2014 | Audit |\n\n### Table: `voice_profiles` (NET-NEW \u2014 biometric PII, RLS own)\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | FK | Description |\n|--------|------|----------|---------|-----|------------|\n| `id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | \u2014 | Primary key |\n| `user_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | `auth.users.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | Tenant isolation (RLS own) |\n| `project_id` | `uuid` | NULL | \u2014 | `projects.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | Project scope (opcional) |\n| `provider` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('elevenlabs','cartesia')` |\n| `clone_method` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('pvc','ivc','voice_design')` \u2014 OTD-AC-002 (PVC/v2 ou IVC/v3, nunca \"PVC+v3\") |\n| `voiceprint_vault_ref` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Voiceprint cifrado no Vault (UUID do secret, nunca valor em claro) |\n| `language` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('en','pt-BR')` \u2014 idioma \u00daNICO por perfil (FM-AC-013 code-switch bloqueado) |\n| `status` | `text` | NOT NULL | `'active'` | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('active','revoked')` |\n| `last_used_at` | `timestamptz` | NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Drive do retention-sweep por inatividade |\n| `created_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | \u2014 | Audit |\n| `updated_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | \u2014 | Audit |\n\n### Table: `avatar_consents` (NET-NEW \u2014 imut\u00e1vel, LGPD Art. 11)\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | FK | Description |\n|--------|------|----------|---------|-----|------------|\n| `id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | \u2014 | Primary key |\n| `user_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | `auth.users.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | Titular biom\u00e9trico (RLS own) |\n| `purpose` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `CHECK IN ('train_lora','face_embedding','voice_clone')` \u2014 finalidade **separada** com checkbox pr\u00f3prio (LGPD Art. 11) |\n| `term_version` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Vers\u00e3o do termo aceito (trilha de auditoria) |\n| `consent_granted` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `true` s\u00f3 com checkbox espec\u00edfico; nunca leg\u00edtimo interesse/contrato |\n| `ip_address` | `inet` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | IP do aceite (prova material LGPD) |\n| `attestation_titular` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Atestado #1 (F1) \u2014 user confirma ser titular da pr\u00f3pria conta/chave |\n| `attestation_commercial_tier` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Atestado #2 (F1) \u2014 tier do user permite uso comercial (Cartesia/Higgsfield) |\n| `revoked_at` | `timestamptz` | NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `null` = ativo; preenchido = render fail-closed (a \u00daNICA coluna mut\u00e1vel) |\n| `created_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | \u2014 | Timestamp imut\u00e1vel do consentimento |\n\n### Table: `video_renders` (REUSO + ALTER \u2014 ledger de render, OTD-AC-005)\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | FK | Description |\n|--------|------|----------|---------|-----|------------|\n| *(colunas existentes preservadas \u2014 `id`/`user_id`/`org_id`/`status`/`engine`/`composition`/`mco_charged`/\u2026)* | | | | | Migration `20260624120000` (`composition` jsonb + bucket) j\u00e1 existe |\n| `composition.avatar` *(jsonb branch \u2014 NET)* | `jsonb` | NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `{ avatar_identity_id, voice_profile_id, source_type, input_script, c2pa_embedded, disclosure_marker, autopilot }` |\n| `engine` *(CHECK relaxado)* | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | `+'hedra' / 'omnihuman' / 'kling'` adicionados ao enum existente (`hyperframes`, \u2026) |\n\n> **`composition.avatar.source_type` \u2208 `('footage_real','avatar_ai')`** dirige o roteamento de motor (footage_real\u2192OmniHuman re-lip-sync; avatar_ai\u2192Hedra/Kling \u2014 F3). `c2pa_embedded`/`disclosure_marker` s\u00e3o **gates n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1veis de publish** (Art. 50(2)/(4)).\n\n### Table: `avatar_reply_log` (NET-NEW \u2014 reply engine do X, pay-per-use ledger, RLS own)\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | FK | Description |\n|--------|------|----------|---------|-----|------------|\n| `id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | `gen_random_uuid()` | \u2014 | Primary key |\n| `user_id` | `uuid` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | `auth.users.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | Tenant isolation (RLS own) |\n| `mention_id` | `text` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | ID do tweet/mention respondido (dedup) |\n| `reply_tweet_id` | `text` | NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | ID do reply postado (null at\u00e9 POST ok) |\n| `has_link` | `boolean` | NOT NULL | `false` | \u2014 | reply-com-link ($0.20) vs engajamento ($0.015) |\n| `mco_charged` | `int` | NOT NULL | `0` | \u2014 | Custo deduzido via RPC (`CONTENT_COST` texto / `AVATAR_VIDEO_COST` se v\u00eddeo) |\n| `dedup_window_utc` | `date` | NOT NULL | \u2014 | \u2014 | Janela UTC de dedup 24h (UNIQUE com `user_id`+`mention_id`) |\n| `created_at` | `timestamptz` | NOT NULL | `now()` | \u2014 | Audit |\n\n### Table: `user_api_keys` (REUSO + ALTER \u2014 BYOK colunas, Vault-encrypted)\n\n| Column | Type | Nullable | Default | Vault key name | Description |\n|--------|------|----------|---------|----------------|------------|\n| `hedra_api_key` | `text` | NULL | \u2014 | `user_api_keys_hedra_api_key_<user_id>` | BYOK Hedra Character-3 (talking-head) \u2014 402 se null |\n| `omnihuman_api_key` | `text` | NULL | \u2014 | `user_api_keys_omnihuman_api_key_<user_id>` | BYOK OmniHuman 1.5 (\u2261 `fal_api_key`) \u2014 re-lip-sync footage real |\n| `elevenlabs_api_key` *(J\u00c1 EXISTE)* | `text` | NULL | \u2014 | `user_api_keys_elevenlabs_api_key_<user_id>` | BYOK voz (migration `20260622210000` j\u00e1 carrega) \u2014 confirmar/reusar |\n| `cartesia_api_key` | `text` | NULL | \u2014 | `user_api_keys_cartesia_api_key_<user_id>` | BYOK voz real-time Sonic \u2014 402 se null |\n| `higgsfield_api_key` *(J\u00c1 EXISTE)* | `text` | NULL | \u2014 | `user_api_keys_higgsfield_api_key_<user_id>` | BYOK Soul ID/Kling (migration `20260527005356`) \u2014 N\u00c3O recriar |\n\n> **Padr\u00e3o Vault (recipe `20260622210000`):** valor real cifrado em `vault.secrets` via `vault_upsert_secret`; coluna armazena o UUID do secret; **masked view** mostra `\u2022\u2022\u2022\u2022`; **INSTEAD OF** = UPDATE-first/INSERT-if-not-found + guard de tenant `auth.uid()`; **decrypted view** (service-role only) faz LEFT JOIN com `vault.decrypted_secrets`. **Magnific N\u00c3O entra aqui** \u2014 \u00e9 org-key system-level (`MAGNIFIC_ORG_API_KEY` env), n\u00e3o per-user (F1 / OTD-AC-011).\n\n---\n\n## 3. Supabase Physical Schema Stubs\n\n```sql\n-- ============================================================\n-- MIGRATION: 20260701120000_avatar_clone_ai_biometric.sql\n-- /security-review OBRIGAT\u00d3RIO antes do commit (FMEA-011 cross-tenant biometric leak \u2014 FM-AC-011).\n-- ============================================================\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 avatar_identities (biometric PII \u2014 RLS own default-deny) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TABLE public.avatar_identities (\n  id                    uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id               uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  project_id            uuid REFERENCES public.projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  persona_type          text     NOT NULL CHECK (persona_type IN ('self_clone','fictional')),\n  tier                  smallint NOT NULL CHECK (tier IN (1,2,3)),\n  identity_storage_key  text     NOT NULL,                 -- bucket privado, prefixo user_id/\n  provider              text     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'higgsfield'\n                                 CHECK (provider IN ('higgsfield','lora_flux')),\n  arcface_threshold     numeric  NOT NULL,                 -- calibrado FAR/FRR/EER (OTD-AC-004 \u2014 NUNCA hardcode)\n  verification_passed   boolean  NOT NULL DEFAULT false,   -- true s\u00f3 ap\u00f3s identity-verification gate (FR-AC-032)\n  commercial_license_ok boolean  NOT NULL DEFAULT false,   -- gate FM-AC-001\n  embedding             vector(768),                       -- mesh embedding (trigger embed-mcorch-node)\n  status                text     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'training'\n                                 CHECK (status IN ('training','active','revoked')),\n  last_used_at          timestamptz,                       -- drive do retention-sweep por inatividade\n  created_at            timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at            timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 voice_profiles (biometric PII \u2014 RLS own default-deny) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TABLE public.voice_profiles (\n  id                   uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id              uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  project_id           uuid REFERENCES public.projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  provider             text NOT NULL CHECK (provider IN ('elevenlabs','cartesia')),\n  clone_method         text NOT NULL CHECK (clone_method IN ('pvc','ivc','voice_design')),\n  voiceprint_vault_ref text NOT NULL,                      -- cifrado no Vault (UUID do secret, nunca valor)\n  language             text NOT NULL CHECK (language IN ('en','pt-BR')),  -- 1 idioma/perfil (FM-AC-013)\n  status               text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active' CHECK (status IN ('active','revoked')),\n  last_used_at         timestamptz,\n  created_at           timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at           timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 avatar_consents (imut\u00e1vel \u2014 LGPD Art. 11) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TABLE public.avatar_consents (\n  id                          uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id                     uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  purpose                     text    NOT NULL CHECK (purpose IN ('train_lora','face_embedding','voice_clone')),\n  term_version                text    NOT NULL,\n  consent_granted             boolean NOT NULL,             -- true s\u00f3 com checkbox espec\u00edfico\n  ip_address                  inet    NOT NULL,\n  attestation_titular         boolean NOT NULL,             -- atestado #1 (F1)\n  attestation_commercial_tier boolean NOT NULL,             -- atestado #2 (F1)\n  revoked_at                  timestamptz,                  -- null=ativo; preenchido=fail-closed (\u00fanica coluna mut\u00e1vel)\n  created_at                  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 avatar_reply_log (X reply engine pay-per-use ledger \u2014 RLS own) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TABLE public.avatar_reply_log (\n  id               uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id          uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  mention_id       text    NOT NULL,\n  reply_tweet_id   text,\n  has_link         boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,          -- $0.20-com-link vs $0.015 engajamento\n  mco_charged      int     NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  dedup_window_utc date    NOT NULL,\n  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  CONSTRAINT avatar_reply_log_dedup UNIQUE (user_id, mention_id, dedup_window_utc)  -- dedup 24h UTC\n);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 RLS shells (default-deny \u2014 biometric own; DELETE s\u00f3 via erase RPC) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nALTER TABLE public.avatar_identities ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nALTER TABLE public.voice_profiles    ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nALTER TABLE public.avatar_consents   ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nALTER TABLE public.avatar_reply_log  ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\n-- avatar_identities: own SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE; DELETE s\u00f3 via erase_avatar_artifacts (SECURITY DEFINER).\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_identities_select_own\" ON public.avatar_identities\n  FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_identities_insert_own\" ON public.avatar_identities\n  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_identities_update_own\" ON public.avatar_identities\n  FOR UPDATE USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n-- (sem policy DELETE direta \u2014 default-deny; remo\u00e7\u00e3o s\u00f3 pelo erase RPC tenant-guarded)\n\n-- voice_profiles: id\u00eantico padr\u00e3o own (DELETE s\u00f3 via erase RPC).\nCREATE POLICY \"voice_profiles_select_own\" ON public.voice_profiles\n  FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"voice_profiles_insert_own\" ON public.voice_profiles\n  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"voice_profiles_update_own\" ON public.voice_profiles\n  FOR UPDATE USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- avatar_consents: SELECT/INSERT own; UPDATE s\u00f3 de revoked_at (imut\u00e1vel \u2014 trigger enforce abaixo).\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_consents_select_own\" ON public.avatar_consents\n  FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_consents_insert_own\" ON public.avatar_consents\n  FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_consents_revoke_own\" ON public.avatar_consents\n  FOR UPDATE USING (user_id = auth.uid()) WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- avatar_reply_log: SELECT own; writes via edge fn service-role (default-deny \u21d2 no INSERT policy).\nCREATE POLICY \"avatar_reply_log_select_own\" ON public.avatar_reply_log\n  FOR SELECT USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 Immutability trigger (avatar_consents \u2014 bloqueia UPDATE de qualquer coluna != revoked_at) \u2500\u2500\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_avatar_consent_immutable() RETURNS trigger\n  LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = '' AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - 'revoked_at') IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) - 'revoked_at') THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'avatar_consents \u00e9 imut\u00e1vel (s\u00f3 revoked_at pode mudar)' USING errcode = '42501';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND $$;\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_avatar_consent_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON public.avatar_consents\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.enforce_avatar_consent_immutable();\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 Indexes (B-tree tenant + HNSW mesh similarity) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE INDEX avatar_identities_user_idx        ON public.avatar_identities (user_id);\nCREATE INDEX avatar_identities_status_used_idx ON public.avatar_identities (status, last_used_at);  -- retention sweep\nCREATE INDEX voice_profiles_user_idx           ON public.voice_profiles (user_id);\nCREATE INDEX voice_profiles_status_used_idx    ON public.voice_profiles (status, last_used_at);\nCREATE INDEX avatar_consents_user_purpose_idx  ON public.avatar_consents (user_id, purpose, revoked_at);  -- consent gate\n\n-- HNSW (embedding can\u00f4nico \u2014 mesh cosine similarity, dims 768 \u2014 SSOT CLAUDE.md Memory Layer):\nCREATE INDEX avatar_identities_embedding_hnsw\n  ON public.avatar_identities USING hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops)\n  WITH (m = 16, ef_construction = 64);\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 embed-mcorch-node trigger (mesh node no insert da identidade) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TRIGGER trg_avatar_identities_embed AFTER INSERT ON public.avatar_identities\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.trigger_embed_mcorch_node();\n\n-- \u2500\u2500 updated_at triggers \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\nCREATE TRIGGER set_avatar_identities_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.avatar_identities\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\nCREATE TRIGGER set_voice_profiles_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON public.voice_profiles\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.update_updated_at_column();\n```\n\n```sql\n-- ============================================================\n--              -- service_role passa user via row-scope (n\u00e3o body \u2014 OTD-VA-008)\n  v_storage_keys text[] := '{}';\n  v_key text;\nBEGIN\n  -- Tenant guard: cada DELETE re-escopado por user_id (poisoned id N\u00c3O vira primitivo cross-tenant \u2014 FM-AC-011).\n  IF p_avatar_identity_id IS NOT NULL THEN\n    SELECT identity_storage_key INTO v_key FROM public.avatar_identities\n      WHERE id = p_avatar_identity_id AND (user_id = v_user_id OR auth.role() = 'service_role');\n    IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'avatar_identity_not_found' USING errcode = 'P0002'; END IF;\n    v_storage_keys := array_append(v_storage_keys, v_key);\n    DELETE FROM public.avatar_identities WHERE id = p_avatar_identity_id\n      AND (user_id = v_user_id OR auth.role() = 'service_role'); GraphRAG Knowledge Nodes\n\n### Node Types Introduced\n\n| node_type | Title Pattern | Content Fields | Embedding Source |\n|-----------|--------------|----------------|-----------------|\n| `avatar_render_observation` | `Avatar render \u2014 <persona_type> <engine> <render_id>` | `engine` \u00b7 `source_type` \u00b7 `mco_charged` \u00b7 `persona_type` \u00b7 `language` \u00b7 `disclosure_marker` \u00b7 `c2pa_embedded` \u00b7 resumo do `input_script` | `title + script_summary` (`embed-mcorch-node`) |\n| `avatar_identity_lock` | `Avatar identity lock \u2014 Tier<tier> <persona_type>` | `tier` \u00b7 `arcface_threshold` calibrado \u00b7 `verification_passed` \u00b7 `provider` \u00b7 `commercial_license_ok` | `title + persona descriptor` |\n| `avatar_brand_voice_dna` | `Brand Voice DNA \u2014 <user vanity>` | \"Brand Voice DNA\" quantificado (autoridade X/10, entusiasmo Y/10) \u2014 System Prompt do c\u00e9rebro-clonado (RAG, N\u00c3O fine-tune) | `quantified voice descriptor` |\n\n> O `avatar_brand_voice_dna` \u00e9 o **substrato RAG do `avatarScript`** (c\u00e9rebro clonado \u2014 blueprint \u00a71): recuperado por similaridade sobre `mcorch_nodes` para ancorar sa\u00eddas em material aprovado (corta alucina\u00e7\u00e3o). Os n\u00f3s de avatar s\u00e3o **per-user** (`user_id = auth.uid()` \u2014 strict isolation), nunca system nodes.\n\n### Edge Types Introduced\n\n| Edge relationship | Source node_type | Target node_type | Metadata |\n|-------------------|-----------------|-----------------|----------|\n| `RENDERED_WITH_IDENTITY` | `avatar_render_observation` | `avatar_identity_lock` | `{ render_id, identity_passed_gate }` |\n| `SCRIPT_GROUNDED_IN` | `avatar_render_observation` | `avatar_brand_voice_dna` | `{ rag_top_k, similarity }` |\n| `IDENTITY_DERIVED_FROM_CONSENT` | `avatar_identity_lock` | *(consent ref \u2014 logical)* | `{ consent_id, purpose, term_version }` |\n\n### Embedding Trigger\n\nTodo insert em `avatar_identities` (e o n\u00f3 de observa\u00e7\u00e3o no 1\u00ba render bem-sucedido) chama `embed-mcorch-node` (CLAUDE.md Memory Layer \u2014 OpenRouter `text-embedding-3-small`, truncado a `vector(768)`, \u00edndice HNSW cosine):\n\n```typescript\n// Na Edge Function, ap\u00f3s o insert / 1\u00ba render bem-sucedido (Mesh Connection Mandate):\nawait supabase.functions.invoke('embed-mcorch-node', {\n  body: { node_id: insertedNode.id, content: `${title} ${scriptSummary}` }\n})\n```\n\n### FSRS-6 Parameters\n\n| Field | Initial Value | Decay Rule |\n|-------|--------------|------------|\n| `stability_score` | `0.5` | Decai pela f\u00f3rmula da migration `20260425190000` (FSRS-6); n\u00f3s de avatar herdam o decaimento do mesh \u2014 n\u00f3s de render raramente acessados decaem; `avatar_brand_voice_dna` (recuperado a cada `avatarScript`) ganha estabilidade |\n| `revenue_impact` | derivado de `creative_metrics.hook_rate` + 1\u00aa convers\u00e3o de marca atribu\u00edda ao post (North-Star OTD-AC-013) | Atualizado a cada render bem-sucedido que entra na cad\u00eancia do Viral Autopilot |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Tenant Isolation Map\n\n| Table | `user_id` scope | `project_id` scope | System rows (`user_id IS NULL`) | DELETE path |\n|-------|----------------|-------------------|-------------------------------|-------------|\n| `avatar_identities` | \u2705 Required (RLS own) | Optional | **No** (PII biom\u00e9trica \u2014 nunca system) | S\u00f3 `erase_avatar_artifacts` (SECURITY DEFINER tenant-guarded) |\n| `voice_profiles` | \u2705 Required (RLS own) | Optional | **No** | S\u00f3 `erase_avatar_artifacts` |\n| `avatar_consents` | \u2705 Required (RLS own) | n-a | **No** | CASCADE de `auth.users` (LGPD Art. 16/18 via erase) \u2014 sem DELETE direto |\n| `avatar_reply_log` | \u2705 Required (RLS own) | n-a | **No** | CASCADE de `auth.users` |\n| `video_renders` (REUSO) | \u2705 Required (own OR org via `is_org_member`) | via `composition` | No | Existente |\n| `creative_assets` (REUSO) | \u2705 Required (own OR org) | via `org_id` | No | DELETE own (registry row; bytes via Storage API) |\n| `user_api_keys` (REUSO) | \u2705 Required (`auth.uid() = user_id` na view + INSTEAD OF guard) | n-a | No | RPC disconnect (revoga segredo Vault) |\n| `autopilot_plans` (REUSO) | \u2705 Required (RLS own) | n-a | No | Existente (RESTRICTIVE no-delete) |\n| `mcorch_nodes` (REUSO \u2014 avatar nodes) | \u2705 Required (per-user; avatar nodes NUNCA `user_id IS NULL`) | Optional | Yes (system nodes gen\u00e9ricos \u2014 mas avatar nodes s\u00e3o per-user) | Cascade via erase RPC (edges\u2192nodes) |\n\n> **Invariante anti-conta-mestra (F1 / OTD-AC-011):** toda resolu\u00e7\u00e3o de BYOK e todo render escopam `auth.uid()`; nenhum artefato biom\u00e9trico \u00e9 system-row; nenhuma chave per-user serve outro tenant. Grep audit zero fan-out \u00e9 gate t\u00e9cnico (FR-AC-006). **`Storage isolation`:** bucket privado `video-studio-assets` com **prefixo `user_id/`** \u2014 signed URL curta owner-scoped (FR-AC-026 / NFR-AC-013).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Data Quality Rules\n\n| Table | Column | Rule | Violation Message (PT-BR) |\n|-------|--------|------|--------------------------|\n| `avatar_identities` | `verification_passed` | Render bloqueado enquanto `false` p/ `persona_type='self_clone'` (gate identity-verification \u2014 FR-AC-032) | \"Verifica\u00e7\u00e3o de identidade pendente \u2014 conclua o gate de selfie antes de gerar o avatar.\" |\n| `avatar_identities` | `arcface_threshold` | `NOT NULL` e calibrado emp\u00edrico; **rejeitar** valor hardcoded 0.95/0.7-0.8 sem proveni\u00eancia de calibra\u00e7\u00e3o (OTD-AC-004) | \"Limiar do Identity Gate n\u00e3o calibrado \u2014 execute a calibra\u00e7\u00e3o FAR/FRR antes de ativar.\" |\n| `avatar_identities` | `commercial_license_ok` | `false` \u21d2 pesos non-commercial (FLUX.1-dev) N\u00c3O podem render (FM-AC-001) | \"Modelo base sem licen\u00e7a comercial \u2014 selecione um checkpoint licenciado.\" |\n| `voice_profiles` | `language` | 1 idioma por perfil; s\u00edntese com code-switch PT\u2194EN intra-frase \u00e9 bloqueada na edge fn (FM-AC-013) | \"Idioma misto n\u00e3o suportado \u2014 gere PT-BR e EN em perfis separados.\" |\n| `voice_profiles` | `voiceprint_vault_ref` | Deve ser UUID de secret Vault v\u00e1lido; NUNCA valor de voiceprint em claro | \"Voiceprint inv\u00e1lido \u2014 credencial biom\u00e9trica deve estar cifrada no cofre.\" |\n| `avatar_consents` | `consent_granted` | Insert exige `true` + `purpose` espec\u00edfico; render fail-closed se faltar consent ativo para a finalidade | \"Consentimento ausente para esta finalidade \u2014 autorize o uso biom\u00e9trico antes de continuar.\" |\n| `avatar_consents` | `purpose` | As 3 finalidades (`train_lora`/`face_embedding`/`voice_clone`) s\u00e3o checkboxes **separados** (LGPD Art. 11 \u2014 espec\u00edfico/destacado) | \"Consentimento gen\u00e9rico n\u00e3o \u00e9 v\u00e1lido \u2014 confirme cada finalidade individualmente.\" |\n| `avatar_consents` | (linha) | Imut\u00e1vel exceto `revoked_at` (trigger `enforce_avatar_consent_immutable`) | \"Registro de consentimento \u00e9 imut\u00e1vel \u2014 s\u00f3 a revoga\u00e7\u00e3o pode alter\u00e1-lo.\" |\n| `avatar_reply_log` | `(user_id, mention_id, dedup_window_utc)` | UNIQUE \u2014 dedup 24h UTC; nunca responder a mesma mention 2\u00d7 na janela (FR-AC-043) | \"Men\u00e7\u00e3o j\u00e1 respondida nesta janela \u2014 reply duplicado bloqueado.\" |\n| `video_renders` | `composition.avatar.disclosure_marker` | `true` obrigat\u00f3rio no publish (Art. 50(4)) \u2014 render sem disclosure \u00e9 bloqueado no gate de publica\u00e7\u00e3o n\u00e3o-pul\u00e1vel (FM-AC-004) | \"Marca de divulga\u00e7\u00e3o de IA obrigat\u00f3ria \u2014 publica\u00e7\u00e3o bloqueada sem o selo.\" |\n| `video_renders` | `composition.avatar.source_type` | `\u22651 footage_real` na cad\u00eancia (postura h\u00edbrida \u2014 FM-AC-014); 100%-avatar bloqueado fail-closed | \"Postura h\u00edbrida exige rosto real peri\u00f3dico \u2014 configura\u00e7\u00e3o 100%-avatar n\u00e3o permitida.\" |\n| `user_api_keys` | `hedra/omnihuman/cartesia_api_key` | Per-user fail-closed; null \u21d2 402 `<service>_not_configured` no fluxo user-facing (API Tenancy Model) | \"Credencial do provedor n\u00e3o configurada \u2014 adicione sua chave em /dashboard/settings.\" |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Retention & Archival Policy\n\n> **Base legal (F6 / `00c \u00a7F6`):** face embeddings (ArcFace), voiceprints e LoRA-de-pessoa = **dado pessoal sens\u00edvel** (LGPD Art. 5 II + 11; GDPR Art. 9). **N\u00c3O existe prazo num\u00e9rico autoritativo** \u2014 a ANPD ainda n\u00e3o publicou regulamento vinculante de reten\u00e7\u00e3o de biom\u00e9trico. A regra material \u00e9 **\"estritamente necess\u00e1rio + eliminar ao t\u00e9rmino\" (Art. 16)**. Qualquer \"X dias\" \u00e9 **CONFIG de engenharia, n\u00e3o exig\u00eancia legal** (parametriz\u00e1vel; sugest\u00e3o de partida 90d de inatividade).\n\n| Data | Retention Period | Archival Strategy | Deletion Trigger |\n|------|-----------------|-------------------|-----------------|\n| `avatar_identities` (LoRA weights + ArcFace embedding) | Enquanto `status='active'`; sweep ap\u00f3s **N dias de inatividade** (config, default 90d via `last_used_at`) | **Sem arquivamento** \u2014 biom\u00e9trico n\u00e3o se arquiva, elimina-se (Art. 16) | (a) revoke de consent \u2192 `erase_avatar_artifacts` imediato (fail-closed); (b) `avatar-retention-sweep` cron di\u00e1rio por inatividade; (c) request de elimina\u00e7\u00e3o do titular (Art. 18) |\n| `voice_profiles` (voiceprint Vault) | Idem `avatar_identities` | Sem arquivamento; voiceprint revogado no Vault + delete na API de voz terceira | Idem \u2014 `erase_avatar_artifacts` + revoga\u00e7\u00e3o Vault + notifica\u00e7\u00e3o ElevenLabs/Cartesia (Art. 18) |\n| `avatar_consents` | **Indefinido** (prova de base legal \u2014 trilha de auditoria); `revoked_at` preenchido N\u00c3O apaga a linha (registro hist\u00f3rico do consentimento que existiu) | Mantido como evid\u00eancia LGPD; nunca arquivado fora do tenant | CASCADE s\u00f3 com a conta (`auth.users` ON DELETE CASCADE) |\n| Storage biom\u00e9trico (bucket `video-studio-assets`, prefixo `user_id/`) | Espelha o artefato dono | Bucket privado; signed URL curta; **Storage API remove** (n\u00e3o SQL \u2014 OTD-VM-026) | Disparado pela edge fn `erase-avatar-artifacts` / `avatar-retention-sweep` com as `storage_keys` retornadas pelo RPC |\n| C\u00f3pias em motores terceiros (Hedra/OmniHuman/fal.ai/ElevenLabs/Cartesia) | Fora do controle do MCORCH | n-a | **Notifica\u00e7\u00e3o a terceiros (Art. 18)** \u2014 best-effort + reconcilia\u00e7\u00e3o eventual \u226424h (NFR-AC-008); cada delete logado |\n| `video_renders` (output MP4) + `creative_assets` | Conte\u00fado publicado (n\u00e3o biom\u00e9trico per se); reten\u00e7\u00e3o de produto | Registry aponta para bucket existente;
